<div class="about-site">
<h3 class="more">More info about this Join My Band site...</h3>
<p>Join My Band is simply a free musicians classifieds. I'm Steve, and I launched the site in October 2006 after having difficulty finding a free, simple and effective way to find a band to join online. Since then Join My Band has grown rapidly, with thousands of musicians using the site every day, and the site now generating (as of Janaury 2013) over 2 million page views a month.</p>
<p>It's great hearing from members who've found themselves a band through the site, so if you have had success with Join My Band, or do so in the future, do <a href="/contact.php">get in touch</a> and let me know, and if you're not a member, why not <a href="/classifieds/login.php">sign up</a> and post an ad - it only takes a few minutes and it's free to do.</p>
<p>Thousands of drummers, guitarists, bassists, vocalists and other musicians visit Join My Band every day, so what are you waiting for?     
<a href="/classifieds/login.php">Sign up now</a>!</p>
<p></p>
<p>Good luck!</p>

<div class="cl_flex_header"><h3>Purposes</h3></div><div class="cl_flex_body"><p></p><label class="cl_toggle_check"><span class="cl_toggle_all select_all text_deselect" style="display:none;">Disable all</span><span class="cl_toggle_all select_all text_select" style="">Select all</span><input class="cl_checkbox" style="display:none" type="checkbox"/><div class="cl_check_indicator"><span></span></div></label><div class="cl_textbox"><h5>Information storage and access</h5><p>The storage of information, or access to information that is already stored, on your device such as advertising identifiers, device identifiers, cookies, and similar technologies.</p><label class="cl_toggle_check"><input class="cl_checkbox cl_purpose_checkbox" style="display:none" type="checkbox" value="1"/><div class="cl_check_indicator"><span></span></div></label></div><div class="cl_textbox"><h5>Personalisation</h5><p>The collection and processing of information about your use of this service to subsequently personalise advertising and/or content for you in other contexts, such as on other websites or apps, over time. Typically, the content of the site or app is used to make inferences about your interests, which inform future selection of advertising and/or content.</p><label class="cl_toggle_check"><input class="cl_checkbox cl_purpose_checkbox" style="display:none" type="checkbox" value="2"/><div class="cl_check_indicator"><span></span></div></label></div><div class="cl_textbox"><h5>Ad selection, delivery, reporting</h5><p>The collection of information, and combination with previously collected information, to select and deliver advertisements for you, and to measure the delivery and effectiveness of such advertisements. This includes using previously collected information about your interests to select ads, processing data about what advertisements were shown, how often they were shown, when and where they were shown, and whether you took any action related to the advertisement, including for example clicking an ad or making a purchase. This does not include personalisation, which is the collection and processing of information about your use of this service to subsequently personalise advertising and/or content for you in other contexts, such as websites or apps, over time.</p><label class="cl_toggle_check"><input class="cl_checkbox cl_purpose_checkbox" style="display:none" type="checkbox" value="3"/><div class="cl_check_indicator"><span></span></div></label></div><div class="cl_textbox"><h5>Content selection, delivery, reporting</h5><p>The collection of information, and combination with previously collected information, to select and deliver content for you, and to measure the delivery and effectiveness of such content. This includes using previously collected information about your interests to select content, processing data about what content was shown, how often or how long it was shown, when and where it was shown, and whether the you took any action related to the content, including for example clicking on content. This does not include personalisation, which is the collection and processing of information about your use of this service to subsequently personalise content and/or advertising for you in other contexts, such as websites or apps, over time.</p><label class="cl_toggle_check"><input class="cl_checkbox cl_purpose_checkbox" style="display:none" type="checkbox" value="4"/><div class="cl_check_indicator"><span></span></div></label></div><div class="cl_textbox"><h5>Measurement</h5><p>The collection of information about your use of the content, and combination with previously collected information, used to measure, understand, and report on your usage of the service.
